\section{Functional Requirements}\label{sec:srs-9}
\texttt{AC}-01 verifies the exact stated behaviour; \texttt{AC}-02 verifies applicable rejection/\allowbreak{}permission paths. Apply only actions specified by the individual \texttt{FR}; the wider use case supplies context. Criteria are specifications, not recorded test results.

Name & Register \\[3pt]
Description & Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Register” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & An unverified registration and \texttt{OTP} delivery outcome are recorded; no access/\allowbreak{}refresh tokens are issued before verification. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Customers register with email, password, full name and an optional phone number. The system sends a verification \texttt{OTP} and issues no token yet. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Sign in \\[3pt]

Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Sign in” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Sign in with email and password to get an access token (\texttt{JWT}, 60 minutes) and a refresh token (7 days). An unverified email gets \texttt{403} \texttt{EMAIL\_\allowbreak{}NOT\_\allowbreak{}VERIFIED\allowbreak{}}. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & Successful verified authentication stores the returned user/\allowbreak{}tokens and selects the allowed role landing; failure grants no session. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}02-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Sign in with email and password to get an access token (\texttt{JWT}, 60 minutes) and a refresh token (7 days). Name & Verify email \texttt{OTP} \\[3pt]
Description & Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Verify email \texttt{OTP}” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & Successful verified authentication stores the returned user/\allowbreak{}tokens and selects the allowed role landing; failure grants no session. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}07-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Email verification with a 6-digit \texttt{OTP}: expires after 5 minutes, at most 5 attempts, 60 seconds between sends, a new code replaces the old one. Verification must include the registered password; on success the user is signed in and gets a welcome email. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}07-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
Evidence & \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Controllers/AuthController.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}ers/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}er.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_Mobile/lib/features/auth/screens/verify\_email\_screen.dart}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}Mobile/\allowbreak{}lib/\allowbreak{}features\allowbreak{}/\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}screens/\allowbreak{}verify\_\allowbreak{}email\_\allowbreak{}screen.\allowbreak{}dart}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Service/AuthService.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/ApiGateway/ApiGateway/Program.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Program.\allowbreak{}cs}} \\[3pt]

Name & Account field validation \\[3pt]
Description & Mobile validates email (required, max 150, no spaces, domain suffix at least two letters), new passwords (6–72 \texttt{UTF}-8 bytes), name (required, max 150) and optional phone (8–15 digits, max 20 characters). Registration/\allowbreak{}new-password forms confirm the password; server field errors return \texttt{400} and are shown to the user. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Account field validation” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Mobile validates email (required, max 150, no spaces, domain suffix at least two letters), new passwords (6–72 \texttt{UTF}-8 bytes), name (required, max 150) and optional phone (8–15 digits, max 20 characters). Registration/\allowbreak{}new-password forms confirm the password; server field errors return \texttt{400} and are shown to the user. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]

Registration/\allowbreak{}new-password forms confirm the password; server field errors return \texttt{400} and are shown to the user. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}AUTH-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Movie reviews \\[3pt]
Description & Display a movie’s average, star distribution and newest reviews. A customer can create/\allowbreak{}update a 1–5 star review with at most 1,000 comment characters from their checked-in ticket, one review per customer per movie, and delete their own review. A ticket not checked in returns \texttt{400}; another owner’s ticket returns \texttt{404}. Display full name or masked email. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest/\allowbreak{}Customer \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A movie is available; changes need Admin; a review needs the owner’s checked-in ticket. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Movie reviews” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Display a movie’s average, star distribution and newest reviews. A customer can create/\allowbreak{}update a 1–5 star review with at most 1,000 comment characters from their checked-in ticket, one review per customer per movie, and delete their own review. A ticket not checked in returns \texttt{400}; another owner’s ticket returns \texttt{404}. Display full name or masked email. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Show empty results for no matching films/\allowbreak{}showtimes. Review of another user’s ticket returns \texttt{404}; a ticket not checked in returns \texttt{400}. Failed media must retain a poster/\allowbreak{}fallback. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}MOV-\allowbreak{}12-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Display a movie’s average, star distribution and newest reviews. A customer can create/\allowbreak{}update a 1–5 star review with at most 1,000 comment characters from their checked-in ticket, one review per customer per movie, and delete their own review. A ticket not checked in returns \texttt{400}; another owner’s ticket returns \texttt{404}. Display full name or masked email. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}MOV-\allowbreak{}12-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Screening format \\[3pt]
Description & Display showtime format 2D/\allowbreak{}3D/\allowbreak{}\texttt{IMAX} beside the room and use server \texttt{seat\allowbreak{}Prices}. The shared backend adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} for 3D or 40,000 \texttt{VND} for \texttt{IMAX} to the 2D standard price before applying \texttt{VIP}/\allowbreak{}sweetbox surcharges. No mobile showtime editor is in scope. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest/\allowbreak{}Customer; Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin read details only during check-in \\[3pt]
Preconditions & Movie and room exist; management changes need Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Screening format” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Display showtime format 2D/\allowbreak{}3D/\allowbreak{}\texttt{IMAX} beside the room and use server \texttt{seat\allowbreak{}Prices}. The shared backend adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} for 3D or 40,000 \texttt{VND} for \texttt{IMAX} to the 2D standard price before applying \texttt{VIP}/\allowbreak{}sweetbox surcharges. No mobile showtime editor is in scope. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject overlap with \texttt{409} and invalid time/\allowbreak{}format with \texttt{400}; cancelled showtimes are final. Customer selection excludes closed or started shows. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The requested authorized data/\allowbreak{}state is returned or displayed; a read does not alter booking/\allowbreak{}payment state. Notification read markers are changed only by their separate write operation. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}SHOW-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Display showtime format 2D/\allowbreak{}3D/\allowbreak{}\texttt{IMAX} beside the room and use server \texttt{seat\allowbreak{}Prices}. The shared backend adds 20,000 \texttt{VND} for 3D or 40,000 \texttt{VND} for \texttt{IMAX} to the 2D standard price before applying \texttt{VIP}/\allowbreak{}sweetbox surcharges. No mobile showtime editor is in scope. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}SHOW-\allowbreak{}13-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Hold booking \\[3pt]
Description & Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A signed-in customer has selected an \texttt{OPEN} future showtime and seats; counter guests are represented by Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Hold booking” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A concurrent hold returns \texttt{409} and must not charge money or leave partial seats/\allowbreak{}points. Invalid concessions/\allowbreak{}code/\allowbreak{}points return validation errors. Expiry releases seats and restores reserved points. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & One \texttt{PENDING} booking with server total and \texttt{expires\allowbreak{}At} is committed; seats are \texttt{HELD} for ten minutes. A rejected transaction leaves no partial holds or point deduction. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Create a booking from a showtime and seats: the showtime must be \texttt{OPEN} and not started, with at least one seat and no duplicates. The server takes the show time, movie title and prices from \texttt{Movie\allowbreak{}Service}. The booking is \texttt{PENDING} and holds the seats for 10 minutes. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Cancel booking \\[3pt]
Description & Cancel a booking: an unpaid one is cancelled and its seats released; a paid one follows \texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}BR-\allowbreak{}17}. A customer cancelling a paid ticket creates a refund request: the booking becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and keeps its seats until staff handle it. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer \\[3pt]
Preconditions & Customer is signed in and owns the relevant ticket; the requested action must be permitted by ticket state and time. Staff access applies only where stated. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Cancel booking” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Cancel a booking: an unpaid one is cancelled and its seats released; a paid one follows \texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}BR-\allowbreak{}17}. A customer cancelling a paid ticket creates a refund request: the booking becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and keeps its seats until staff handle it. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A concurrent hold returns \texttt{409} and must not charge money or leave partial seats/\allowbreak{}points. Invalid concessions/\allowbreak{}code/\allowbreak{}points return validation errors. Expiry releases seats and restores reserved points. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}06-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Cancel a booking: an unpaid one is cancelled and its seats released; a paid one follows \texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}BR-\allowbreak{}17}. A customer cancelling a paid ticket creates a refund request: the booking becomes \texttt{REFUND\_\allowbreak{}PENDING} and keeps its seats until staff handle it. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}06-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Promotion and voucher \\[3pt]

Validate active dates, show weekday, minimum ticket count and usage limit. Show the quote in summary and savings on ticket/\allowbreak{}payment; the server recalculates on booking and counts usage only after payment. Invalid/\allowbreak{}ineligible codes return \texttt{400} with a reason. \\[3pt]

Show the quote in summary and savings on ticket/\allowbreak{}payment; the server recalculates on booking and counts usage only after payment. Invalid/\allowbreak{}ineligible codes return \texttt{400} with a reason. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]

Name & Share and calendar \\[3pt]
Description & Paid tickets have \textbf{Share ticket} (movie, show time, seats and ticket code sent to friends through the device's share sheet; copied to the clipboard where the browser cannot share) and \textbf{Add to calendar} (a Google Calendar event in Vietnam time ending when the show ends; only for shows still to come). \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer \\[3pt]
Preconditions & Customer is signed in and owns the relevant ticket; the requested action must be permitted by ticket state and time. Staff access applies only where stated. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Share and calendar” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Paid tickets have \textbf{Share ticket} (movie, show time, seats and ticket code sent to friends through the device's share sheet; copied to the clipboard where the browser cannot share) and \textbf{Add to calendar} (a Google Calendar event in Vietnam time ending when the show ends; only for shows still to come). 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A concurrent hold returns \texttt{409} and must not charge money or leave partial seats/\allowbreak{}points. Invalid concessions/\allowbreak{}code/\allowbreak{}points return validation errors. Expiry releases seats and restores reserved points. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The requested authorized data/\allowbreak{}state is returned or displayed; a read does not alter booking/\allowbreak{}payment state. Notification read markers are changed only by their separate write operation. \\[3pt]
Priority & L \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}19-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Paid tickets have \textbf{Share ticket} (movie, show time, seats and ticket code sent to friends through the device's share sheet; copied to the clipboard where the browser cannot share) and \textbf{Add to calendar} (a Google Calendar event in Vietnam time ending when the show ends; only for shows still to come). \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}BOOK-\allowbreak{}19-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Name & Create/\allowbreak{}reuse \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} link \\[3pt]
Description & Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. One payment per booking; asking again returns the existing link. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS}/\allowbreak{}system as external/\allowbreak{}shared dependencies \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A payable booking exists; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} credentials and callback/\allowbreak{}return settings are configured for live payment. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Create/\allowbreak{}reuse \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} link” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. One payment per booking; asking again returns the existing link. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}PAY-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Create a \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} payment link for a \texttt{PENDING} booking. One payment per booking; asking again returns the existing link. The \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} order code is the booking id \ensuremath{\times} 1,000,000 plus the six random digits that end the booking code: stable per booking and never repeating an order of another environment on the shared \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} account. The link expires with the seat hold. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}PAY-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]

Calling it again is safe. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS}/\allowbreak{}system as external/\allowbreak{}shared dependencies \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A payable booking exists; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} credentials and callback/\allowbreak{}return settings are configured for live payment. \\[3pt]

Calling it again is safe. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]

Name & Refund access \\[3pt]

Actor & Customer; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS}/\allowbreak{}system as external/\allowbreak{}shared dependencies \\[3pt]
Preconditions & A payable booking exists; \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} credentials and callback/\allowbreak{}return settings are configured for live payment. \\[3pt]

Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Approval, rejection and recording bank transfers belong to the web workspace, not the mobile application. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]

Name & Signed webhook \\[3pt]
Description & Receive the \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} webhook: check the \texttt{HMAC}-\texttt{SHA256} signature (\texttt{400} when wrong); an unknown order or wrong amount returns 200 and is ignored; a repeated webhook confirms and emails nothing twice. The payer's account (number, name, bank) is stored for refunds. \\[3pt]
Actor & Shared backend system; customer receives outcome \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The corresponding committed event, trusted \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} result or scheduled-worker condition exists; backend processing is configured. No customer session is required for a background worker or signed webhook. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Signed webhook” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: Receive the \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} webhook: check the \texttt{HMAC}-\texttt{SHA256} signature (\texttt{400} when wrong); an unknown order or wrong amount returns 200 and is ignored; a repeated webhook confirms and emails nothing twice. The payer's account (number, name, bank) is stored for refunds. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]

Name & Late payment \\[3pt]

Actor & Shared backend system; customer receives outcome \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The corresponding committed event, trusted \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} result or scheduled-worker condition exists; backend processing is configured. No customer session is required for a background worker or signed webhook. \\[3pt]

Execute this operation: When \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} reports payment after the hold expired, confirm the booking only if all seats can be reclaimed; otherwise create a full refund request. Repeated verification/\allowbreak{}webhook must not duplicate financial effects. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Never trust redirect query parameters as proof of payment. Handle cancellation, timeout and \texttt{503} without showing false success; late payment confirms only if seats can be reclaimed, otherwise creates a refund. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]

Name & Create refund request \\[3pt]

The mobile customer sees the resulting request and notification; the phone does not initiate an administrative refund. \\[3pt]
Actor & Customer \\[3pt]

The mobile customer sees the resulting request and notification; the phone does not initiate an administrative refund. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & A cancellation inside two hours is refused with \texttt{409}. Display backend refund/\allowbreak{}rejection states; the phone provides no approve/\allowbreak{}reject/\allowbreak{}transfer-record action. \\[3pt]

Name & Camera/\allowbreak{}manual scanner \\[3pt]
Description & Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin scan a ticket \texttt{QR} with the phone camera or type/\allowbreak{}paste its code and submit. Camera denial/\allowbreak{}unavailability must leave manual lookup usable. This does not expose web counter or management screens. \\[3pt]
Actor & Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin \\[3pt]
Preconditions & Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin is signed in; the ticket is \texttt{CONFIRME\allowbreak{}D}, unused and within 180 minutes before to 120 minutes after start. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Camera/\allowbreak{}manual scanner” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin scan a ticket \texttt{QR} with the phone camera or type/\allowbreak{}paste its code and submit. Camera denial/\allowbreak{}unavailability must leave manual lookup usable. This does not expose web counter or management screens. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Keep manual entry available if camera is unavailable/\allowbreak{}denied. Unknown code returns \texttt{404}; unpaid, cancelled, pending refund, out-of-window or already-used ticket returns \texttt{409}. Concurrent second check-in fails. Optional metadata failure must not hide the ticket. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & Only one check-in timestamp is stored; check-in is an attribute, not another booking status. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}CHK-\allowbreak{}04-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Staff/\allowbreak{}Admin scan a ticket \texttt{QR} with the phone camera or type/\allowbreak{}paste its code and submit. Name & Showtime reminder \\[3pt]
Description & \textbf{Showtime reminders}: a background job (every minute, \texttt{Showtime\allowbreak{}Reminder\allowbreak{}} settings) emails and records a notification for paid tickets not checked in whose show starts within 2 hours; each ticket once. Tickets bought inside that window are skipped (the customer just got the confirmation). \\[3pt]
Actor & Shared backend system; customer receives outcome \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The corresponding committed event, trusted \texttt{Pay\allowbreak{}OS} result or scheduled-worker condition exists; backend processing is configured. No customer session is required for a background worker or signed webhook. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Showtime reminder” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: \textbf{Showtime reminders}: a background job (every minute, \texttt{Showtime\allowbreak{}Reminder\allowbreak{}} settings) emails and records a notification for paid tickets not checked in whose show starts within 2 hours; each ticket once. Tickets bought inside that window are skipped (the customer just got the confirmation). 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Owner checks reject another user’s data; show loading/\allowbreak{}empty/\allowbreak{}error-retry states. Sending failure retains \texttt{FAILED} for retry and must not undo payment. Event deduplication reduces duplicates but \texttt{SMTP} plus database commit is not a guaranteed exactly-once transaction. \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The requested authorized data/\allowbreak{}state is returned or displayed; a read does not alter booking/\allowbreak{}payment state. Notification read markers are changed only by their separate write operation. \\[3pt]
Priority & M \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NOTI-\allowbreak{}08-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: \textbf{Showtime reminders}: a background job (every minute, \texttt{Showtime\allowbreak{}Reminder\allowbreak{}} settings) emails and records a notification for paid tickets not checked in whose show starts within 2 hours; each ticket once. Tickets bought inside that window are skipped (the customer just got the confirmation). \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NOTI-\allowbreak{}08-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
Evidence & \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/BookingService/BookingService/Controllers/NotificationController.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}ers/\allowbreak{}Notifica\allowbreak{}tion\allowbreak{}Controll\allowbreak{}er.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/BookingService/BookingService/Services/Implementations/NotificationService.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Services\allowbreak{}/\allowbreak{}Implemen\allowbreak{}tations/\allowbreak{}Notifica\allowbreak{}tion\allowbreak{}Service.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/BookingService/BookingService/Workers/ShowtimeReminderWorker.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Booking\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Workers/\allowbreak{}Showtime\allowbreak{}Reminder\allowbreak{}Worker.\allowbreak{}cs}} \\[3pt]

Name & Route guards and recovery \\[3pt]
Description & Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. \\[3pt]
Actor & Guest; signed-in account; system \\[3pt]
Preconditions & The account form is reachable and gateway/\allowbreak{}\texttt{Auth\allowbreak{}Service} are configured; no prior signed-in session is required for registration/\allowbreak{}login/\allowbreak{}\texttt{OTP}/\allowbreak{}recovery. \texttt{SMTP} is required when sending a code. \\[3pt]
Trigger & Invoke “Route guards and recovery” or its specified backend event/\allowbreak{}worker. \\[3pt]
Main flow & 1. Read current input/\allowbreak{}resource and the applicable session. 2. Execute this operation: Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. 3. Return/\allowbreak{}render the authoritative result and refresh affected data after success. \\[3pt]
Alternative /\allowbreak{} exception flow & Reject invalid fields (\texttt{400}), bad credentials (\texttt{401}), unverified email (\texttt{403}), stale/\allowbreak{}incorrect \texttt{OTP}, or resend/\allowbreak{}rate limits (\texttt{429}). Clear the session for account-lock/\allowbreak{}role-change responses; show \texttt{SMTP}/\allowbreak{}dependency failure (\texttt{503}). \\[3pt]
Postconditions & The successful observable result matches this requirement; any specified state change is persisted. Rejection does not grant access or leave an unauthorized partial change. \\[3pt]
Priority & H \\[3pt]
Acceptance criteria & \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NAV-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}01}} — Given the preconditions/\allowbreak{}trigger, verify: Guard named routes: a signed-out protected destination opens login with its pending route; successful sign-in resumes an allowed destination. Unknown routes fall back to the role landing. A summary lacking its in-memory \texttt{Booking\allowbreak{}Draft} returns to seat selection. \textbf{\texttt{MOB-\allowbreak{}FR-\allowbreak{}NAV-\allowbreak{}01-\allowbreak{}AC-\allowbreak{}02}} — Exercise applicable invalid/\allowbreak{}unauthorized/\allowbreak{}conflicting input and verify the stated rejection, no unauthorized changes and no false success. \\[3pt]
Evidence & \href{../../../Cinema\_Mobile/lib/features/auth/screens/login\_screen.dart}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}Mobile/\allowbreak{}lib/\allowbreak{}features\allowbreak{}/\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}screens/\allowbreak{}login\_\allowbreak{}screen.\allowbreak{}dart}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/AuthService/AuthService/Service/AuthService.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Service/\allowbreak{}Auth\allowbreak{}Service.\allowbreak{}cs}}; \href{../../../Cinema\_BE/ApiGateway/ApiGateway/Program.cs}{\texttt{Cinema\_\allowbreak{}BE/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Api\allowbreak{}Gateway/\allowbreak{}Program.\allowbreak{}cs}} \\[3pt]
